## `AGO-2710-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Impulse-Voltage Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Switchgear Power-Frequency Withstand & Lightning Impulse Test
- **Intro:** Impulse-Voltage Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Switchgear Power-Frequency Withstand & Lightning Impulse Test — Pre-test Safety and Environmental Verification: Temperature: 23±2°C; Relative humidity: 45±5%; Grounding resistance: ≤0.1 Ω; Safety interlock continuity: ≤0.5 Ω

- **Standards:** `IEC 60060-1:2010`, `IEC 60060-2:2011`, `IEC 62271-1:2017`, `ISO 10012:2003`
